Motivation: 现有ASR模型在异构标注数据上训练时，转录风格（逐字vs.意图）作为未控制的潜变量，导致解码不稳定、评估混淆（多达60%的WER归因于风格不匹配）以及词级时间戳不可靠，需解决风格控制问题。

Method: 1) 覆盖感知解码器任务令牌在平行逐字/意图转录对上训练；2) 完整英语微调；3) 监督交叉注意力微调改进词级时间戳；4) 提出verbatimize新任务用于大规模创建规范逐字转录。

Result: 零样本下将德语不流畅度F1从10%提升至79%（仅英语训练）；完整英语微调在逐字准确率、不流畅检测和意图模式质量上超越所有基线；交叉注意力微调在不流畅语音上改进了词级时间戳。

Conclusion: 通过显式控制转录风格，可显著提升ASR模型在逐字与意图模式下的性能，并解决评估混淆和时间戳不可靠问题。verbatimize任务为大规模高质量语音语料库构建提供了新途径。

Abstract: Modern ASR models trained on heterogeneously annotated data treat transcription style (verbatim vs. intended) as an uncontrolled latent variable, causing measurable decoding instability, evaluation confounding (up to 60% of reported WER attributable to style mismatch), and unreliable word-level timing. We show that models already encode both styles; the challenge is controlled activation. Using coverage-aware decoder task tokens trained on parallel verbatim/intended transcript pairs, we raise German disfluency F1 from 10% to 79% zero-shot, despite English-only training. Full English-only fine-tuning surpasses all baselines in verbatim accuracy, disfluency detection, and intended-mode quality across both languages. We further introduce supervised cross-attention fine-tuning that improves word-level timestamps on disfluent speech beyond forced-alignment baselines. Finally, we propose verbatimize, a new task enabling scalable creation and enrichment of speech corpora with high-quality canonical verbatim transcriptions.

</details>


### [105] [Constrained CTC Decoding for Efficient Diacritic Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18946)
*Rufael Marew,Amr Keleg,Hanan Aldarmaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CTC的非自回归语音到文本变音符恢复方法，通过构建字符级变音符格子在解码时引入硬约束，在古典阿拉伯语和现代标准阿拉伯语测试集上显著降低了变音符错误率，同时相比多模态基线方法提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数阿拉伯语语音数据的转录文本缺少变音符（diacritics），这限制了细粒度语音区分的建模能力。现有文本变音符恢复方法效果有限，而语音模态作为补充信息来源被探索，但现有方法计算复杂度较高。

Method: 提出一种基于CTC的非自回归语音到文本变音符恢复方法。核心在于解码时引入硬约束：从未加变音符的转录文本构建字符级变音符格子（lattice），限制解码假设仅为有效的变音符实现。

Result: 在ArVoice（古典阿拉伯语）和ClArTTS（现代标准阿拉伯语）测试集上，与计算复杂度更高的多模态变音符恢复基线相比，本方法在两个数据集上均取得了统计显著的变音符错误率降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在性能和效率上均优于基线，证明了非自回归CTC方法结合解码时硬约束的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we address diacritic restoration for Arabic speech transcripts. Most speech data are undiacritized, limiting the ability of modeling fine-grained phonological distinctions. The speech modality has recently been explored as a way to complement text-based diacritic restoration efforts. We propose an efficient non-autoregressive approach for speech-to-text diacritization based on Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC). Our method incorporates hard constraints during decoding by constructing a character-level diacritization lattice from an undiacritized transcript and restricting hypotheses to valid diacritized realizations. We evaluate on Classical Arabic and Modern Standard Arabic test sets (namely, ArVoice and ClArTTS) against a more computationally-complex multi-modal diacritic restoration baseline, and show statistically significant reductions in diacritic error rates in both, demonstrating that the proposed approach offers both performance and efficiency gains.

</details>


### [106] [Verifiable Self-Evolution for Open-Ended Dialogue Skills via Future-Feedback Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18973)
*ChaoJin Zhao,Xuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出未来反馈技能进化方法，将对话中不可验证的响应评估转化为可验证的用户信号预测任务，实现离线文本技能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放域对话中，助手响应的改变会导致用户后续反应变化，因此无法用固定的日志数据直接评估反事实响应，缺乏稳定的验证信号来支持技能自我进化。

Method: 将技能进化目标从规定当前答案转向预测当前响应是否会导致正面或负面的后续用户信号，使预测任务可在固定日志元组上验证，从而支持基于验证的文本优化。

Result: 在专有隐私保护的销售助手数据集上，经过严格质量过滤和平衡的已解决/未解决分割，预测准确率超过75%。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是将动态变化的对话反馈转化为固定的离线学习目标，使技能进化可复现而无需将每个候选技能部署到在线流量中；该方法定位为离线优化阶段，而非替代最终人工或在线评估。

Abstract: Textual skills provide a lightweight way to improve frozen language-model agents, but their self-evolution normally requires a stable validation signal. Such signals are natural in mathematics or code, where an answer can be checked after it changes, yet are problematic in open-ended dialogue: changing the assistant response also changes the user's next reaction, so a logged reaction cannot directly evaluate a counterfactual response. We propose future-feedback skill evolution, which first redirects self-evolution from prescribing the current answer to predicting whether the observed answer will lead to a positive or negative subsequent user signal. This prediction task is verifiable on fixed logged tuples and therefore supports validation-gated textual optimization. The evolved feedback skill captures interpretable criteria for response quality and can subsequently serve as a diagnostic and optimization target for answer skills. On a proprietary, privacy-preserving sales-assistant dataset, careful quality filtering and a balanced resolved/unresolved split yield more than 75% prediction accuracy. Beyond this result, the central contribution is a formulation that converts otherwise moving conversational feedback into a fixed offline learning target, enabling reproducible skill evolution without placing every candidate skill in live traffic. We discuss the boundary between observational verification and counterfactual validity, and position the method as an offline optimization stage rather than a replacement for final human or online evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [AutoJourn: Multi-Perspective Summarisation, Bias Detection and Bias Neutralisation for LLM-Generated News in Automated Journalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18983)
*Himel Ghosh,Ahmed Mosharafa,Georg Groh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoJourn是一个用于多视角新闻生成和偏见感知评估的演示系统，通过大语言模型从社交媒体中提取多元观点、生成平衡摘要，并检测/中和新闻中的偏见，配有公开可用的在线演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动化新闻中三个核心挑战：从非结构化社交媒体讨论中提取多元视角、生成保留观点多样性的摘要、检测和缓解AI生成新闻中的偏见，以推动负责任的自动化新闻发展。

Method: 集成先进提示工程与可选检索增强生成语义多样的视角集合；多视角摘要模块将冲突观点合并为平衡摘要；偏见分析套件支持句子级偏见检测、类型分类和自动中性化改写。用户可在界面中检查视角聚类、比较立场特定摘要、生成新闻文章并应用偏见感知重写。

Result: 在语义多样性、摘要质量和偏见减少等内在指标上优于强基线方法，同时保持内容忠实性。

Conclusion: AutoJourn为负责任的自动化新闻提供了有效的多视角生成与偏见评估框架，公开演示促进了可复现性和后续研究。

Abstract: We present AutoJourn, a demonstration system for multi-perspective news generation and bias-aware evaluation using large language models (LLMs). The system tackles three core challenges in responsible automated journalism: extracting diverse perspectives from unstructured social media discussions, generating summaries that preserve viewpoint diversity, and detecting or mitigating bias in AI-generated news. The pipeline integrates advanced prompt engineering with optional retrieval augmentation to produce semantically diverse perspective sets, a multi-perspective summarisation module that merges conflicting viewpoints into balanced summaries, and a bias analysis suite supporting sentence-level bias detection and type classification in the generated news article, and automatic neutralisation. Users can inspect perspective clusters, compare stance-specific summaries, generate news articles, and apply bias-aware rewrites directly in the interface. We evaluate each component with intrinsic metrics -- semantic diversity, summary quality, and bias reduction and show improvements over strong baselines while maintaining content fidelity. A live, publicly accessible demo accompanies the paper to facilitate reproducibility and further research on socially responsible automated journalism.

</details>


### [108] [Disentangling Curriculum Learning in NLP: Towards a Unifying Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18984)
*Vanessa Toborek,Florian Seiffarth,Sebastian Müller,Tamás Horváth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对NLP领域课程学习（CL）研究缺乏统一理论框架的问题，提出了一种细粒度分类法，将难度评估与训练调度分离，揭示了不同CL策略在难度概念和调度机制上的系统不可比性，并提供了设计、分析和比较CL策略的规范化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管课程学习在NLP领域已有十余年研究，但缺乏一个原则性的框架来解释针对特定问题应该使用哪种难度函数或调度器，这阻碍了该领域形成统一的理论认识。

Method: 提出细粒度分类法，将课程学习拆分为难度评估（区分归因来源和任务依赖性，揭示难度作为视角性概念的本质）和训练调度（首次形式化CL调度器的预期训练贡献，引入保留机制和单调性属性）两个独立维度，并对NLP领域中的CL工作进行系统分析。

Result: 发现了系统性的不可比问题：已有工作混淆了不同的难度概念和调度机制，往往在相同"课程学习"标签下追求不同目标，阻碍了比较和连贯证据基础的积累。分类法支持CL策略的设计、分析和比较，并推动能解耦改进来源的评估实践。

Conclusion: 该分类法不仅诊断了NLP领域CL研究中的根本性问题，还提供了规范化的分析工具，有助于推动CL研究的可比较性和可累积性发展。

Abstract: Despite more than a decade of curriculum learning (CL) research in NLP, the field lacks a principled account of which difficulty function or scheduler to use for a given problem. To understand what has hindered progress towards this account, we propose a fine-grained taxonomy separating difficulty evaluation from training scheduling to enable systematic analysis of CL strategies. For difficulty evaluation, we distinguish attribution source and task dependence, revealing difficulty as a perspectival concept encoding different assumptions about what makes an instance hard to learn. For scheduling, we provide the first formalisation of CL schedulers in terms of expected training contribution, enabling comparison across implementations by introducing retention regimes and monotonicity properties. Applied in a dedicated analysis of CL works in NLP, our taxonomy reveals a systematic incomparability problem: prior works conflate distinct notions of difficulty and scheduling, often pursuing different objectives under the same CL label -- hindering comparison and the accumulation of a coherent evidence base. Beyond diagnosis, the taxonomy supports the design, analysis, and comparison of CL strategies, and motivates evaluation practices that disentangle the sources of observed improvement.

</details>


### [109] [MedDDC-Eval: Diagnosis-Decoupled Evaluation of Multi-Turn Medical Consultation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18999)
*Guofeng Zhang,Yizeng Quan,Huaiyi Fang,Jianwei Lv,Jinyao Liu,Xunxu Duan,Lening An,Yu Ouyang,Junfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedDDC-Eval，一种将诊断生成与历史采集解耦的医疗对话评估框架。通过冻结共享阅读器固定历史到诊断的映射，并引入D/T/E指标衡量诊断有用性、信息获取和效率。实验表明，仅更换诊断阅读器可使F1变化2.2-19.0分，并逆转18%-36%的政策排序。基于GRPO对Qwen3-32B进行后训练，在Record和Dialogue分片上分别提升9.7和4.6总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗多轮对话评估将策略采集的历史与策略特定的终端诊断生成耦合在一起，强大的生成器可以补偿薄弱的历史，而弱生成器会掩盖丰富的历史，导致评估结果混淆，无法准确衡量对话策略的采集质量。

Method: 提出MedDDC-Eval解耦测试平台，将采集的历史作为比较对象，通过共享冻结阅读器固定历史到诊断的映射。设计D/T/E评估框架（诊断有用性、信息获取、效率），采用方向性语义覆盖加确定性一一分配实现精确率-召回率计数。使用GRPO对Qwen3-32B进行交互式多轮后训练。

Result: 固定历史仅更换诊断阅读器，诊断F1变化2.2-19.0分，18%和36%的成对政策排序在Record和Dialogue分片上被逆转。GRPO训练后政策在100例Record和70例Dialogue分片上分别提升9.7和4.6总分，去除任一主要信号会降低联合性能。

Conclusion: MedDDC-Eval支持可控归因、可解释的历史采集测量，以及评估引导的证据采集策略开发，有效解耦了历史采集质量与诊断生成能力的评估混淆。

Abstract: Multi-turn medical consultation agents must decide what to ask, adapt to patient responses, and determine when the collected evidence is sufficient. However, coupled evaluation conflates the quality of the policy-elicited history with policy-specific terminal diagnosis generation: strong generation can compensate for a thin history, while weaker generation can obscure a rich one. We introduce MedDDC-Eval, a diagnosis-decoupled testbed that treats elicited history as the comparison object and holds the history-to-diagnosis mapping constant through a shared frozen reader. Across two held-out sources, a grounded interface and an auditable diagnosis-trajectory-efficiency (D/T/E) harness measure diagnostic usefulness, information acquisition, and efficiency. Directional semantic coverage followed by deterministic one-to-one assignment yields coherent precision-recall counts for open-ended items, with at most one credited match per prediction or reference. Holding histories fixed, changing only the diagnostic reader shifts diagnosis F1 by 2.2-19.0 points and reverses 18% and 36% of pairwise policy orderings on the Record and Dialogue splits. We further apply standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) over interactive multi-turn rollouts to post-train Qwen3-32B using diagnosis-result and trajectory feedback. On the 100-case Record and 70-case Dialogue splits, the trained policy improves over its initialization by 9.7 and 4.6 total-score points; removing either primary signal lowers held-out joint performance. These results show that MedDDC-Eval supports controlled attribution, interpretable elicited-history measurement, and evaluation-guided evidence-acquisition policy development.

</details>


### [110] [Computational Humor with Multimodal LLMs: Methods, Datasets, Evaluation, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19011)
*Tuo Liang,Zhe Hu,Disheng Liu,Jing Li,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态视觉幽默理解领域的研究进展，涵盖从识别、解释推理到生成的能力层次，梳理了从任务特定融合模型到基于多模态对齐、证据推理和可控生成的大模型范式的演变，并指出了评估捷径、文化覆盖有限、证据基础薄弱以及安全与所有权问题等主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模因、漫画等媒介中的多模态幽默对AI系统具有独特挑战，因为其含义依赖非字面机制、共享文化知识和交际意图，而非字面场景描述。现有调查未能充分覆盖这一快速发展的领域，因此需要一份聚焦视觉幽默理解的系统性综述。

Method: 采用以能力为中心的分层体系（识别→解释推理→生成）组织文献，综合梳理基准设计、评估协议和建模范式，并追踪从任务特定融合模型到基于多模态对齐、证据推理和可控生成的大模型方法的演变路径。

Result: 识别出该领域的主要进展障碍：捷径驱动的评估（模型利用虚假相关性而非真正理解）、文化和叙事覆盖有限、证据基础薄弱，以及未解决的安全和所有权问题。

Conclusion: 多模态幽默理解已从任务特定模型演进到大模型范式，但面临评估鲁棒性、文化包容性、证据推理和伦理安全等关键挑战，是值得持续关注的前沿方向。

Abstract: Multimodal humor in memes, cartoons, and comics remains difficult for AI systems because intended meaning depends on non-literal mechanisms, shared cultural knowledge, and communicative intent rather than literal scene description. This survey focuses on visual humor understanding in single-image and multi-panel artifacts, while treating humor generation as an emerging downstream frontier. We position the literature against prior humor, sarcasm, and general MLLM surveys and organize it using a capability-centric hierarchy spanning recognition, interpretation and reasoning, and generation. Under this lens, we synthesize benchmark design, evaluation protocols, and modeling paradigms, tracing the field's shift from task-specific fusion models to large-model approaches based on multimodal alignment, evidence-grounded reasoning, and controlled generation. We conclude by highlighting the main barriers to progress: shortcut-prone evaluation, limited cultural and narrative coverage, weak evidence grounding, and unresolved safety and ownership concerns.

</details>


### [111] [Content is What Remains: Invariant Speech Tokenization from Parallel Utterances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19033)
*Laurin Wagner,Bernhard Thallinger,Miroslav Stankovic,Mario Zusag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PINT是一种并行不变式分词方法，通过对齐平行语料中的共享语言内容，从SSL模型中蒸馏出语义不变表征，大幅降低条件熵，在说话人不变性、ABX错误率和LM困惑度上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散语音分词器（如HuBERT）虽然旨在分离语义与声学信息，但SSL模型提取的token仍保留了说话人身份、韵律和信道条件等非语言变异，导致熵值过高，影响下游任务的效率与泛化能力。

Method: 提出PINT框架，通过对平行语料（同一词语由不同说话人在不同条件下发音）进行对齐学习，结合数据增强和一致性损失函数，微调SSL编码器，使得相同词语映射到一致的token序列，从而蒸馏出仅保留语言内容的共享残差表征。

Result: 说话人探针准确率从93.1%降至1.2%（相对下降98.7%），ABX错误率降低42%，语言模型困惑度降低27-30%。

Conclusion: 正确的不变性（仅保留语言内容，去除声学变异）是高效学习的关键。PINT tokens保留了帧级时间对齐能力，可作为音频编解码器的直接替代语义目标，优于ASR文本。

Abstract: Discrete speech tokenizers aim to disentangle semantic from acoustic information, yet targets from self-supervised learning (SSL) models like HuBERT retain non-linguistic variation: speaker identity, prosody, and channel conditions leak into the tokens, inflating entropy. Our key insight is that when enough speakers utter the same words under varying conditions, linguistic content is the only shared factor. We propose PINT (Parallel INvariant Tokenization), which fine-tunes an SSL encoder with alignment losses across parallel utterances and augmentations to distill this shared residual. PINT collapses identical words onto consistent token sequences, drastically reducing conditional entropy. Unlike ASR text, PINT tokens preserve frame-level temporal grounding and serve as drop-in semantic targets for audio codecs. Experiments show a 98.7% relative reduction in speaker probe accuracy (93.1% to 1.2%), a 42% lower ABX error rate, and 27-30% lower LM perplexity versus baselines, confirming that the right invariance is key to efficient learning.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmarking Human and Automatic Speech Recognition of Diverse Speech: Initial Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19049)
*Ilse Huisman,Rares Popa,Yuanyuan Zhang,Odette Scharenborg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了最先进的自动语音识别（ASR）系统与荷兰语母语听众在识别多样化语音（荷兰儿童语音、老年语音以及佛兰德语）方面的表现。Google Telephony在ASR系统中表现最佳，且ASR系统在特定情况下与人类听众表现相当甚至更优，但说话者年龄、地区口音和话语长度对性能有影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为人类是语音识别的上限标准，但该假设缺乏充分的实证验证。本文旨在通过系统比较ASR系统与人类听众在多样化语音识别任务上的表现，检验这一假设的有效性。

Method: 选取多种最先进的ASR系统（包括Google Telephony等）与荷兰语母语听众，针对荷兰儿童语音、老年语音及佛兰德语进行识别任务，并对比不同测试集（Jasmin-CGN）对结果的影响。

Result: Google Telephony在ASR系统中表现最优；ASR系统整体与人类听众表现相近，在特定条件下甚至超越人类；说话者年龄、地区口音和话语长度是影响性能差异的关键因素；测试集选择显著影响人机对比的结论。

Conclusion: 人类并非语音识别的绝对上限，ASR系统在多样化语音识别上可与人类匹敌甚至超越。未来研究应着力提升ASR系统对年龄和口音相关声学变异性的鲁棒性，并注意测试集选择对基准评估的影响。

Abstract: Humans are often considered to be the best listeners and seen as the upper-bound performance of automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems. We present a preliminary comparison of the performances of state-of-the-art ASR systems and Dutch native listeners on the recognition of "diverse" speech, specifically Dutch child and older adults' speech and Flemish. Google Telephony outperformed the other ASR systems. Importantly, the ASR systems showed similar performance to the listeners, and in specific cases even outperformed them. Slight performance differences between the listeners and ASR systems were found related to speaker's age and regional accents and utterance length. Future research should focus on making ASR systems more robust to acoustic variability related to aging and regional accents. A comparison of ASR recognition performances on the test stimuli and the full Jasmin-CGN test sets showed the influence of the specific test sets on the conclusions regarding benchmarking human and ASR performance.

</details>


### [113] [DAIS: Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19088)
*Yu Wang,Ming Fan,Xicheng Zhang,Zhiyong Li,Zhihu Wang,Caiyue Xu,Dahai Hu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DAIS框架，通过将教师推理链转化为依赖感知的阶段级QA记录，在训练时引入中间监督信号，显著提升大模型在复杂推理任务上的最终答案准确率，在政策合规基准上最高提升5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的思维链（CoT）监督虽然提供了中间推理步骤，但扁平化的目标优化仅关注单一推理序列，对局部结论如何支撑后续决策提供的监督有限。需要一种能显式建模推理步骤间依赖关系的训练框架。

Method: 将教师模型生成的推理链过滤后转化为阶段级QA记录。每个中间记录基于之前决策所需的状态预测局部答案，最终答案记录保持原始任务格式。评估时仅使用原始输入和可选上下文。

Result: 在GDPR、AIACT、MedQA和FOLIO四个数据集上，使用多种Qwen骨干网络，DAIS相比纯答案、扁平CoT和独立QA基线，平均最终答案准确率均有提升。政策合规基准上，最大提升5.6%，平均提升4.2%。

Conclusion: 有效的先前状态依赖贡献超越了更长目标或额外中间文本的收益，支持依赖条件化的中间QA作为标准最终答案推理的轻量级辅助监督信号。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision exposes intermediate rationales, but flat rationale targets usually optimize a single reasoning sequence and provide limited supervision on how local conclusions should support later decisions. We introduce Dependency-Aware Intermediate QA Supervision (DAIS), a training-time framework that converts filtered teacher rationales into stage-level QA records. Each intermediate record predicts a local answer conditioned on the previous states needed for that decision, while the final-answer record keeps the original task format; evaluation therefore uses only the original input and optional context. Across GDPR, AIACT, MedQA, and FOLIO with multiple Qwen backbones, DAIS improves average final-answer accuracy over answer-only, flat chain-of-thought, and independent-QA baselines. On policy-compliance benchmarks, it achieves a largest gain of 5.6% and an average gain of 4.2% over the strongest non-DAIS baseline. Controlled ablations show that valid previous-state conditioning contributes beyond longer targets or additional intermediate text, supporting dependency-conditioned intermediate QA as a lightweight auxiliary supervision signal for standard final-answer inference.

</details>


### [114] [Translation as Augmentation: Effect of Translated Data on Assessment of Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19101)
*Yiheng Wu,Jue Hou,Roman Yangarber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对低资源语言缺乏专家标注语料库的问题，本文提出了一种跨语言数据增强策略，利用机器翻译将高资源语言的标注数据迁移到低资源语言，并训练BERT回归模型进行文本难度评估。实验表明，使用机器翻译语料增强稀缺的原始数据能显著提升难度估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本难度评估是文本简化和个性化学习的前提，但低资源语言缺乏专家标注的细粒度难度级别语料库（如CEFR），严重制约了稳健评估模型的开发。

Method: 提出跨语言数据增强策略：利用机器翻译将高资源语言的标注资源迁移到目标低资源语言；训练基于BERT的回归模型预测难度分数；探究合成翻译数据是否能有效补充原始训练集。

Result: 使用机器翻译语料增强稀缺的原始数据显著提高了难度估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 跨语言数据增强为缺乏广泛专家标注的低资源语言提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable Text Difficulty Assessment is a prerequisite for valid text simplification workflows and personalized learning applications. However, the development of robust assessment models is severely hindered by a critical bottleneck: the scarcity of expert-annotated corpora containing fine-grained difficulty levels (e.g., CEFR), particularly for lower-resource languages. This paper addresses this data scarcity problem in the context of a low-resource European language. We propose a cross-lingual data augmentation strategy that leverages machine translation to transfer labeled resources from high-resource languages to the target low-resource language. We train BERT-based regression models to predict difficulty scores and investigate whether synthetic, translated data can effectively supplement native training sets. Our experiments demonstrate that augmenting scarce native data with machine-translated corpora significantly improves the accuracy of difficulty estimation, offering a viable solution for languages lacking extensive expert annotations.

</details>


### [115] [Automated Extraction of Techno-Economic Data from 76,000 Energy System Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19178)
*Maxime Gorres,Jan Göpfert,Patrick Kuckertz,Noor Titan Putri Hartono,Heidi Heinrichs,Jochen Linßen,Iain Staffel,Jann Michael Weinand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种从76,000篇能源系统研究中自动提取定量信息的方法，构建了包含320万结构化数据点和2000万元数据条目的FAIR数据库，并提供了交互式仪表盘供用户筛选、分析和下载数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统模型的决策依赖定量假设，但手动提取信息效率低下、难以审计，且数据库更新不及时、研究重复劳动严重。

Method: 采用高精度自动化提取技术，从2010年以来发表的76,000篇能源系统研究中提取定量信息，构建结构化数据库。

Result: 成功提取320万结构化数据点和2000万关联元数据条目，覆盖多种技术、方法论和系统特征，并发现学术假设与实证数据之间的差异以及研究优先级在不同技术、区域和时间上的变化。

Conclusion: 自动化提取方法能显著提升能源系统文献分析的透明度和效率，所构建的数据库不仅为模型提供输入数据，还使文献本身可分析，对推动能源系统建模实践具有重要价值。

Abstract: Energy system models guide societally important decisions, but their credibility rests on quantitative assumptions that are difficult to source and audit. Meta-analyses can improve transparency and modeling practices, but the rapid growth of publications makes manual information extraction increasingly impractical. Consequently, databases are updated infrequently and efforts are often duplicated across research groups. Here, we demonstrate the highly accurate automated extraction of quantitative information from 76,000 energy system studies published since 2010. We compile 3.2 million structured quantitative data points together with 20 million associated metadata entries, spanning a broad spectrum of technologies, methodological approaches and system characteristics. Beyond providing input data for models, the resulting FAIR database make the energy systems literature itself analysable. We show where academic assumptions diverge from empirical observed data, and how research priorities vary at scale across technologies, regions and time. To facilitate broad use within the community, the database is provided through an interactive dashboard, enabling users to filter, analyse and download data according to their specific research needs.

</details>


### [116] [Reasoning Before Translation: Enhancing Legal Machine Translation with Structured Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19181)
*Aixiu An,Michael Jungo,Eloi Eynard,Mark Drenhaus,Andreas Fischer,Jean Hennebert,Sébastien Rumley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对比了多种增强方法（监督微调、强化学习等）在瑞士法律领域神经机器翻译中的效果，发现强化学习优于监督微调，增强后的小模型性能接近但不及前沿推理模型，且重新训练范式随模型规模增大收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的神经机器翻译因法律语言的复杂性和高精度要求而极具挑战性。近期涌现的具推理能力的语言模型为应对此类挑战提供了新可能，但不同增强方法（监督微调、强化学习等）在小模型与前沿推理模型之间的效果对比尚不明确。

Method: 以瑞士法律系统（多语言法规）为测试bed，对Qwen3.5 4B、Qwen3.5 9B、Gemma 3 12B等小语言模型分别采用监督微调和带可验证奖励的强化学习进行重训练增强，并与前沿推理模型的翻译质量进行比较。

Result: 小"基础"模型的质量可大幅提升；强化学习在法域NMT中优于监督微调；增强后的小模型性能接近但仍不及前沿推理模型；重新训练范式随模型规模增大收益递减。

Conclusion: 强化学习是可验证奖励可有效应用于法律领域NMT，为小模型增强提供了优于监督微调的方案，但尚无法完全超越前沿推理模型。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) in the legal domain is a linguistically and conceptually demanding task, primarily due to the complexity of legal language and the high level of precision it requires. The recent emergence of reasoning-capable language models opens new possibilities for tackling such challenges. They add to a set of other previously proposed techniques to enhance the translation quality, which includes supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
  In this work, we perform a comparison between these various approaches. More particularly, we evaluate small language models such as Qwen3.5 4B, Qwen3.5 9B, and Gemma 3 12B enhanced with various re-training paradigms and compare their performances against frontier reasoning models. We focus on the Swiss legal system, which -- with its unique multilingual statutes -- offers a particularly challenging testbed for reasoning-augmented models. Our results show that the quality of small ``base'' models can be greatly enhanced, and that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can be applied to NMT in the legal domain and surpasses the translation quality of supervised fine-tuning. The performance of enhanced small models is close to the one of state-of-the-art reasoning models yet remains inferior. We also note that re-training paradigms yield diminishing returns as model size increase. The code and models are publicly available at https://github.com/aixiuxiuxiu/Legal-MT-SFT-RL.

</details>


### [117] [MIRA-Ev:A Benchmark for Granular Evidence Detection and Relational Reasoning in Clinical Exams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19201)
*Iker De la Iglesia,Johanna Ramirez-Romero,Jose Maria Villa-Gonzalez,Irune Urroz García,Ander Barrena,Aitziber Atutxa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIRA-Ev是一个基于西班牙MIR执业医师考试的临床论证挖掘基准，包含三级任务（证据句检索、论证成分抽取、关系分类），并提供西班牙语、英语和巴斯克语三种版本，旨在超越传统MCQA评估中仅关注最终答案正确性的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCQA评估仅衡量最终答案准确率，无法检测模型是否基于无关、缺失或矛盾的证据得出正确诊断，缺乏对临床推理过程的细粒度评估。

Method: 构建MIRA-Ev基准：基于西班牙MIR考试病例，由临床专家重新标注了span级别的premises、claims及directed support/attack relations；组织为三级任务层次：证据句检索、论证成分抽取和关系分类；提供了西班牙语（原生）、英语和巴斯克语三个平行版本。

Result: 发布了首个临床论证资源（包括巴斯克语版本），并建立了三级任务评估体系，可对模型在临床推理中的证据使用进行细粒度评测。

Conclusion: MIRA-Ev为临床NLP评估提供了超越MCQA的论证级评测手段，有助于检测模型推理过程而非仅关注最终答案。

Abstract: Clinical NLP evaluation remains dominated by multiple-choice question answering (MCQA), which scores only final-answer accuracy and cannot detect when a model reaches the correct diagnosis while grounding it in irrelevant, absent, or contradictory evidence. We introduce MIRA-Ev, a clinical argument mining benchmark built on Spanish Médico Interno Residente (MIR) licensing-exam cases, re-annotated by expert clinicians with span-level premises, claims, and directed support/attack relations, and released in parallel Spanish (native), English, and Basque versions, the first clinical argumentation resource in Basque. MIRA-Ev organizes evaluation into a three-tier task hierarchy: evidence sentence retrieval, argumentative component extraction, and relation classification.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond Score Prediction: LLM-Based Essay Scoring and Feedback Generation via Reinforcement Learning with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19219)
*Xuefeng Jin,Jiashuo Zhang,Teng Cao,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RLAES框架，通过强化学习联合优化作文评分与反馈生成，引入基于量规的反馈评估（RFE）和自适应门控反馈优化（AGFO），在ASAP基准上达到LLM方法最优评分性能（QWK=0.803），同时保持高质量反馈生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在自动作文评分和反馈生成中主要依赖提示工程或监督微调，缺乏对强化学习后训练和反馈质量自动化评估的系统研究。

Method: 1) RLAES：统一LLM框架，通过RL联合优化评分与反馈生成；2) RFE：基于量规的反馈评估框架，包含166个细粒度二元量规项目和LLM-as-judge；3) AGFO：自适应门控反馈优化，按需激活量规奖励，降低评估开销；4) ACR：相邻对比推理，通过显式对比相邻分数级别改进序数评分校准。

Result: ASAP基准上，RLAES-AGFO在LLM方法中取得最优评分性能（QWK=0.803），反馈质量与GPT-5.5相当，且避免了纯评分RL导致的反馈退化问题。

Conclusion: RFE框架能有效捕捉作文-反馈一致性，具有强成对区分能力且与专家偏好高度一致；RL后训练可同时提升评分与反馈质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely applied to automated essay scoring (AES) and automated feedback generation (AFG). However, existing studies rely primarily on prompt engineering or supervised fine-tuning, while systematic research on reinforcement learning (RL) post-training and automated evaluation of feedback quality remains limited. We propose RLAES, a unified LLM framework that jointly optimizes essay scoring and feedback generation through RL. To make feedback quality measurable, interpretable, and usable for training, we introduce Rubric-based Feedback Evaluation (RFE), an essay-grounded feedback evaluation framework comprising 166 fine-grained binary rubric items and an LLM-as-judge. Building on RFE, we propose Adaptive Gated Feedback Optimization (AGFO), which activates rubric-based feedback rewards on demand during RL, reducing evaluation overhead while improving feedback quality. We also propose Adjacent Contrastive Reasoning (ACR) to improve ordinal score calibration by explicitly contrasting adjacent score levels. Experimental results show that the RFE framework captures essay-feedback consistency, exhibits strong pairwise discriminative power, and closely aligns with expert preferences. On the ASAP benchmark, RLAES-AGFO achieves the best scoring performance among LLM-based methods (QWK = 0.803), while maintaining feedback quality comparable to GPT-5.5 and avoiding the feedback degradation observed under score-only RL. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/hellomuyi/RLAES.

</details>


### [119] [The Price of Reasoning: Cost-Quality Tradeoffs in Reinforcement Learning for Neural Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19226)
*Michael Jungo,Aixiu An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究RLVR在LLM后训练中对NMT任务的影响，重点探究推理链(reasoning trace)在训练和推理阶段的必要性。实验表明仅在推理阶段加入推理链即可提升翻译质量，但需权衡计算成本与质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明RLVR是法律文档翻译的首选训练方法，并归因于其诱导的推理能力。本文旨在验证翻译质量的提升是否真正源于推理能力，还是更广泛地源于训练范式本身。

Method: 通过系统性地在训练和/或推理阶段移除模型的推理链(reasoning trace)，设计对比实验探究推理链在不同阶段的必要性。同时分析输出token数量增加带来的计算成本与翻译质量提升之间的权衡。

Result: 仅在推理阶段包含推理链就能显著提升整体翻译质量；推理链导致输出token数量增加，需要在计算成本与翻译质量之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 推理链对翻译质量的提升主要归功于推理阶段的使用，而非训练阶段；实际应用时需根据具体场景评估成本-质量权衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has been established as a viable paradigm for the post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs), including downstream tasks, such as Neural Machine Translation (NMT). With the latest research indicating that RLVR could be the preferred training method for translating legal documents due to the induced reasoning capabilities, it raises the question whether it is really attributed to the reasoning or more generally to the training paradigm. We investigate the importance of including the model's reasoning trace in the generated responses during both training and inference by systematically omitting it from one of the phases. Our experiments show that including the reasoning, specifically during inference, has a positive effect on the overall translation quality. Furthermore, we recognise that the reasoning leads to an increase in output tokens, hence we study the cost-quality tradeoff between the increased computational demands and the improved translation quality.

</details>


### [120] [Selection Shapes the Boundary: A Preregistered Replication of Monotonicity and Label Agreement in Unselected NLI Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19231)
*Haram Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究复现了先前关于NLI中人类标注差异（HLV）与单调性算子关系的发现，证明先前观察到的非向上单调性假设导致更低标注一致性的结论仅存在于低一致性选择的子集中，而非总体人群的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究（arXiv:2607.15870）发现，在ChaosNLI（仅包含多数标签恰好获得三票的项目）中，含有非向上单调性算子的假设显示出更低的标注一致性。本研究旨在检验这一边界是否在SNLI和MultiNLI的总体开发集中同样成立。

Method: 预注册复制研究，使用相同的算子标注器，以四等级序数一致性作为结果变量，在SNLI和MultiNLI开发集（ChaosNLI的源总体）中检验非向上单调性算子与标注一致性的关系。进行了七项对比分析，并进行了鲁棒性检验（模拟标注器误分类、人工重新标注审核）。

Result: 注册预测失败。所有七项对比的Cliff's delta均为正值（非向上单调性假设的一致性略高而非更低），唯一显著的验证性对比与注册符号相反，所有效应量远低于最小感兴趣效应量（0.10）。鲁棒性检验支持测量可靠性：模拟误分类会缩小而非制造效应，人工重新标注审核在200样本上达到四类一致性0.875。

Conclusion: 先前发现的负向边界很可能是以低一致性选择为条件的结构，而非总体层面的属性。基于选择性再标注资源得出的HLV结构主张应明确说明其选择性条件。

Abstract: Prior work on human label variation (HLV) in natural language inference (NLI) has often relied on re-annotation resources that select items by disagreement level. An earlier study (arXiv:2607.15870) found that hypotheses containing non-upward monotonicity operators showed lower label agreement in ChaosNLI (Cliff's delta = -0.284), which is restricted to items whose majority label carries exactly three of five votes. We preregistered a replication of this boundary in the unselected populations that ChaosNLI was drawn from: the SNLI and MultiNLI development sets, using the same operator tagger and a four-level ordinal agreement outcome. The registered prediction fails. All seven contrasts return a positive Cliff's delta (non-upward items agree slightly more, not less), the only significant confirmatory contrast has the opposite sign to the registration, and every effect is far below our smallest effect size of interest (0.10). Robustness checks support the measurement: simulated tagger misclassification shrinks the effects rather than manufacturing them, and a manual re-tagging audit reaches four-class agreement of 0.875 on a fresh 200-item sample. We conclude that the earlier negative boundary is plausibly a structure conditional on low-agreement selection rather than a population-level property, and that HLV structure claims built on selected re-annotation resources should state their selection conditional explicitly.

</details>


### [121] [MeetingToM: Evaluating Multimodal LLMs on Theory-of-Mind Reasoning in Multi-Party Meetings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19235)
*Ziyi Wang,Yuhang Wu,Dongxu Piao,Xingyu Liu,Tianhui Zhou,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MeetingToM是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在多人会议场景中复杂社会行为推理能力的基准测试，重点检测模型在伪共识、隐藏态度推断和群体动态理解等方面的表现。实验表明现有MLLMs在整合非语言线索和区分真实与表面共识方面存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态心智理论基准主要关注基于视频的问答任务，侧重于外部可验证信号，对潜在的社交状态和群体动态覆盖有限，尤其是在多人会议场景中。因此需要构建一个专门针对会议场景中复杂社会行为推理的基准测试。

Method: MeetingToM基准采用层次化组织结构，从三个粒度级别评估心智理论：(i)个体级别的心智状态预测、(ii)双人级别的对话对象理解、(iii)群体级别的共识推理。提供了统一的评估协议，并对代表性MLLMs进行了系统分析。

Result: 代表性MLLMs在整合非语言线索、推断隐藏态度以及区分真实共识与伪共识方面存在持续性的局限性。

Conclusion: MeetingToM揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在多人会议场景中心智推理的关键挑战，为推进多模态模型的会议场景心智理论研究提供了重要测试平台。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer other's beliefs, intentions, and states of knowledge, is central to social interaction, yet remains challenging for current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), especially in multi-party meetings where cues are distributed across speech and behavior. Existing multimodal ToM benchmarks mainly focus on video-grounded question answering over overt, externally verifiable signals, and provide limited coverage of latent social states and group dynamics. We introduce MeetingToM, a benchmark for complex social behavior reasoning in naturalistic multi-party meetings. MeetingToM targets meeting-specific phenomena such as \textbf{pseudo-consensus}, where apparent agreement masks private dissent under social pressure. The benchmark is hierarchically organized to evaluate ToM at increasing levels of social granularity, including (i) subject-level mental state prediction, (ii) dyadic-level addressee understanding, and (iii) group-level consensus reasoning. We provide a unified evaluation protocol and conduct systematic analyses of representative MLLMs, revealing persistent limitations in integrating non-verbal cues, inferring hidden attitudes, and distinguishing genuine consensus from pseudo-consensus. Our results highlight key challenges and establish MeetingToM as a testbed for advancing meeting-grounded ToM in multimodal models.

</details>


### [122] [Inference-Time Steering for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19243)
*Alexander Manev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了大语言模型在多语言环境下的知识表征偏差问题，发现基于英语提示的模型可以通过提示工程（角色提示）在推理时有效对齐目标语言的事实回答，且效果优于内部表征操作（CAA）和权重微调（DPO）等侵入性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理多语言时存在知识表征偏差（偏向高资源语言），导致跨语言事实不一致性——模型仅因提示语言不同就改变答案分布。现有研究缺乏对推理时干预策略的系统比较，尤其是在安全性和泛化性方面的评估。

Method: 在Gemma 3 12B Instruct上测试四种干预策略：零样本上下文引导（角色提示）、对比激活添加（CAA，内部表征操控）、基于基准事实数据的直接偏好优化（DPO）以及概念泛化数据训练。使用多语言事实数据集和自建的文化相关查询泛化基准进行评估。

Result: 角色提示是最强的整体干预策略，在有效性、安全性和域外泛化之间取得最佳平衡。CAA能产生剧烈的不一致性基准偏移，但对配置敏感且存在知识退化风险。DPO适配器提供永久但狭窄且迁移性差的增益。

Conclusion: 跨语言不一致性至少部分是一个选择问题（selection problem），简单上下文干预（角色提示）可能比更侵入性的方法更适合实现鲁棒、可迁移的对齐。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multilingual fluency, their internal knowledge representations remain disproportionately biased toward high-resource languages. This leads to cross-lingual factual inconsistency, where they shift their empirical answer distributions based solely on the prompt language. We investigate whether these biases can be mitigated at inference time, forcing an English-prompted model to answer as if it were queried in target languages (German, Spanish, Bulgarian), and evaluate four intervention strategies: zero-shot contextual steering (persona prompting), internal representation manipulation via Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA), and lightweight weight modification via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) trained on benchmark-derived factual data as well as conceptual generalization data. To assess alignment, we curate a multilingual factual dataset alongside a novel generalization benchmark comprising culturally rooted queries to determine whether factual interventions transfer to broader target-centric preferences. Experiments on Gemma 3 12B Instruct reveal persona prompting to be the strongest overall intervention, balancing efficacy, safety, and out-of-domain generalization. While CAA yields sharp inconsistency benchmark shifts, it is configuration-sensitive and risks knowledge degradation. DPO-based adapters offer permanent, yet narrower and less transferable gains. These findings suggest that cross-lingual inconsistency is at least partly a selection problem, and that simple contextual interventions may outperform more invasive methods for robust, transferable alignment.

</details>


### [123] [Prompt Design at Scale: How Format, Instruction Count, and Context Length Shape Instruction Adherence and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19257)
*Netanel Eliav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过两个大规模对照实验，在无污染合成语料库上系统研究了提示设计的三个关键决策：指令格式、规则数量和上下文长度。实验1发现规则数量达到80条时所有模型完美响应率降至零；实验2发现召回率在64-128K tokens后急剧下降，但编造行为从未发生（0/5760），而模型会在接近上下文极限时直接拒绝回答（0%升至79-90%）。研究还发现格式和位置的影响具有模型特异性，不存在通用的Markdown优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示工程实践中存在三个缺乏受控实验证据支撑的关键设计决策：指令和上下文的格式选择（Markdown、纯文本、散文或表格）、系统提示能承载的并行指令数量上限、以及模型能保持准确回忆和诚实性的上下文长度上限。现有研究缺乏对这些因素的系统性控制实验。

Method: 研究使用"Veyra之书"合成语料库（8780个唯一命名实体，可确定性再生），设计了两个控制实验：实验1（每个模型960次调用）交叉考察规则数量（10-160条）、格式（4种）和放置位置（系统提示vs用户轮次）；实验2（每个模型5520次调用）考察上下文长度（2K-512K tokens）与格式的交互效应，测量召回准确率、虚假前提谄媚和缺失事实编造。

Result: 实验1：所有模型在N=80时完美响应率降至零；放置位置的效果在N=160时与格式相当但方向具有模型特异性；无模型展示出可靠的Markdown优势，一个35B模型反而偏好纯文本。实验2：召回率在64-128K tokens内保持近乎天花板水平后急剧下降，格式间差异最高达48个百分点；编造从未发生（0/5760），谄媚率≤8.3%；接近上下文极限时拒绝回答率从0%飙升至79-90%。格式的token开销（比纯文本高22-37%）进一步影响偏好选择。

Conclusion: 提示设计决策的效果高度依赖于具体模型，不存在通用的最佳格式；规则数量存在明显上限（约80条），超出后性能崩溃；模型在接近上下文极限时主要表现出拒绝而非编造行为，这比幻觉更值得关注；研究发布完整工具链VeyraBench以供复现。

Abstract: Practitioners make three prompt-design decisions with almost no controlled evidence behind them: how to format instructions and context (markdown, plain text, prose, or tabular), how many simultaneous instructions a system prompt can carry before compliance degrades, and how much context a model can hold before recall and honesty degrade. We report two controlled experiments crossing all three factors on one held, contamination-free synthetic corpus (the "Book of Veyra," 8,780 uniquely-named entities, deterministically regenerable from a fixed seed), evaluated across five models.
  Experiment 1 (960 calls/model) measures instruction-following decay as rule count N grows from 10 to 160, crossed with four formats and system-prompt vs. user-turn placement. Perfect-response rate collapses to zero by N=80 for every model, format, and placement. Placement produces effects at least as large as format at N=160 in most models, but the direction is model-specific. No model shows a reliable markdown advantage; one 35B model favors plain text instead.
  Experiment 2 (5,520 calls/model) measures recall accuracy, false-premise sycophancy, and absent-fact fabrication across a 2k-to-512k-token context ladder in the same four formats. Recall stays near ceiling through 64-128k tokens, then degrades sharply and format-dependently: one model's accuracy spread reaches 48 points at 128k tokens. Fabrication never occurs (0/5,760 probes), and sycophancy stays negligible (<=8.3%). What rises sharply near each model's context ceiling is outright refusal to answer (0% to 79-90%), distinct from sycophancy or fabrication. Neither pre-registered format ordering holds, and token overhead (+22% to +37% over plain text) further changes which format is preferable where accuracy spread is genuine.
  We release the full harness, corpus generator, and raw results (VeyraBench): https://github.com/iNetanel/veyrabench

</details>


### [124] [Two-Level Meta-Rubrics for Evaluating Open-Ended Generation: GAMUT, a Benchmark for Factual Completeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19322)
*Xilun Chen,Zhaleh Feizollahi,Ross Goodwin,Seungwhan Moon,Scott Yih,Pinar Donmez,Babak Damavandi,Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长文本生成的事实完整性评估问题，提出了一种双层元评分框架Gamut，通过结构化的元评分板捕获所需内容的组织与重要性，再机械编译为可机器评分的二元检查表，基于1,813个真实可穿戴图像问题评估了14个模型，最佳得分仅58.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本生成的事实性评估仅关注精确率（判断生成claims是否正确），忽视了事实完整性——即回答是否包含所有应含信息。事实完整性评估更难，因为完整答案所需的事实集难以枚举、往往是开放式的、涉及顺序和关系，简单的布尔检查无法捕获。

Method: 提出双层元评分框架：上层是结构化的元评分板（meta-rubric），捕获所需内容的组织和重要性；下层通过机械编译将元评分板转化为由LLM judge可靠评分的二元检查表。构建了1,813个基于真实可穿戴图像的问题，覆盖10个领域，每个问题配有人工专家验证的评分板。框架是模态无关的，同时发布了纯文本变体。

Result: 评估14个前沿和开源模型，Gamut基准具有高挑战性（最佳Gemini 3.1 Pro得分58.7%）、高区分度，且对judge选择鲁棒。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双层评分框架有效解决了开放域长文本生成的事实完整性评估难题，实验表明该基准具有挑战性、区分度和鲁棒性，且框架具有模态通用性。

Abstract: Evaluating the factuality of long-form generations has focused predominantly on precision, measuring whether the claims a model makes are correct. The dominant decompose-search-verify pipeline catches incorrect claims well but says little about whether a response contains all the information it should. Measuring factual completeness, the missing half of factuality, is harder: it requires enumerating the full set of facts a complete answer should contain, and these facts rarely form a flat list. They often involve open-ended sets where coverage is what matters, ordered processes, and relationships among facts that a list of independent boolean checks fails to capture. We introduce a two-level meta-rubric framework for evaluating open-ended generation, and instantiate it as Gamut (Grounded Assessment of Multimodal Factuality), a benchmark for factual completeness in long-form generation. The framework rests on a two-level rubric representation: a structured meta-rubric captures the organization and importance of the required content, which is then mechanically compiled into a flat checklist of binary, machine-gradable rubrics that an LLM judge scores reliably. We construct 1,813 questions grounded in real wearable imagery across 10 diverse domains, each paired with an evidence-backed rubric verified by expert human annotators. Because the framework is modality-agnostic, we also release a text-only variant. Evaluating 14 frontier and open-weight models, we find the benchmark genuinely challenging (best score 58.7% from Gemini 3.1 Pro), highly discriminative, and robust to the choice of judge.

</details>


### [125] [Selective State-Space Adaptation and Retrieval for Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19326)
*Atahan Dokme,Larry Heck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于状态空间模型（Mamba）的两类低秩适配器：MaLoRA（令牌级动态缩放因子）和MaRA（上下文级跨段检索），在三个冻结大模型和两个推理基准上，平均F1提升6.8分（相对+10.5%），最高F1提升9.3分（相对+18.2%），且令牌级改进在长文本压力测试中保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的静态更新无法区分不同输入实例的差异，缺乏对令牌级或实例级状态变化的显式建模，限制了细粒度适配能力。

Method: 提出两类适配器：MaLoRA在令牌级引入Mamba状态空间递归，使缩放因子成为依赖输入和跨令牌递归状态的动态函数；MaRA在上下文级跟踪跨段状态，检索与查询最相关的片段后再生成答案。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B三个冻结模型和MuSiQue、2WikiMultihopQA两个推理基准上，所有6个实验设置均提升，平均F1+6.8（相对+10.5%），最优单元格F1+9.3（相对+18.2%），且MaLoRA在RULER QA-2长文本测试中保持优势。

Conclusion: 在LoRA中引入状态空间递归可以有效提升多跳推理任务的表现，令牌级和上下文级两种粒度的动态适配各有其应用场景和互补优势。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation introduces a static learned update applied identically to every input. The update provides task-level adaptation but does not explicitly represent token-level or instance-level state variation. A family of adapters is proposed that introduces selective state-space recurrence at two complementary granularities. At the token level, \textbf{MaLoRA} (Mamba-modulated low-rank adaptation) makes the adapter's scaling factor a dynamic input-dependent function with recurrent state across tokens, in contrast to the stateless modulators of prior work. At the context level, \textbf{MaRA} (Mamba Retrieval Adapter) tracks cross-segment state and selects the segments most relevant to the query, before the modulated language model generates its answer. Across three frozen backbones (Qwen-2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B) and two reasoning benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultihopQA), the family improves reasoning accuracy on every cell of the $3{\times}2$ grid, by $+6.8$ F1 ($+10.5\%$ relative) on average and up to $+9.3$ F1 ($+18.2\%$ relative) on the hardest cell over the LoRA baseline, and the token-level gains carry to RULER QA-2 under length stress.

</details>


### [126] [Copy Less, Ground More: Overcoming Repetitive Copying in Long-Context Reasoning via Evidence-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19345)
*Lizhe Fang,Weizhou Shen,Tianyi Tang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现长上下文大模型存在“重复复制”问题（从输入中大量复制文本而非有效推理），其根源在于缺乏对关键证据的聚焦。为此提出GEAR（基于证据感知的奖励塑造）方法，在标准准确率奖励基础上增加关键证据重叠的正向奖励和无关上下文的惩罚，通过自动化流水线构建证据标注数据，在多个模型和基准上取得最高+4.6平均点的提升，并减少重复复制和推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM在复杂任务中表现出色，但存在一个关键失败模式：模型在推理链中大量复制输入文本而非进行有效的逐步推理，且该现象随上下文长度加剧。现有方法未有效解决这一问题，因此需要诊断其根因并提出针对性的解决方案。

Method: 1. 首先通过将提示分为任务相关关键证据和无关干扰上下文，诊断出根因是模型不加区分地复制输入，缺乏对关键证据的聚焦。2. 提出GEAR（Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward），在准确率奖励基础上增加：与关键证据重叠的正向奖励（grounding reward）和与无关上下文重叠的惩罚（distractor penalty）。3. 开发自动化流水线，从任意文档中构建带证据标注的训练数据。4. 在多个模型规模和基准上验证效果。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上，GEAR相比基于准确率的标准强化学习，平均提升高达+4.6个点，且更长上下文下增益更大；同时有效减少了重复复制行为和推理长度。

Conclusion: 即使长上下文评估从简单检索转向复杂推理，对相关证据的准确定位仍是不可或缺的能力，且仍有较大改进空间。GEAR通过奖励塑造有效缓解了重复复制问题，提升了模型的推理质量。

Abstract: Large language models that generate step-by-step reasoning traces have achieved strong performance on complex tasks, and extending them to long-context settings has emerged as an important frontier. However, we identify a critical failure mode in this regime: \emph{repetitive copying}, where models extensively copy text from the input into their reasoning traces rather than productively solving the problem. We show that this behavior is pervasive across frontier long-context LLMs and intensifies with context length. By separating each prompt into task-relevant key evidence and irrelevant distractor context, we further show that the root cause is insufficient grounding: models copy from the prompt indiscriminately, and those that fail to focus on key evidence are far more likely to answer incorrectly. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose GEAR (Grounding Evidence-Aware Reward), a reward shaping method that augments the accuracy signal with a grounding reward for overlap with key evidence and a distractor penalty for overlap with irrelevant context. To enable GEAR on natural-language data, we develop an automated pipeline that constructs evidence-annotated training data from arbitrary documents. We validate GEAR across multiple model scales and benchmarks, showing consistent improvements of up to +4.6 average points over standard RL with accuracy-based rewards, with larger gains at longer contexts, while also reducing repetitive copying and thinking length. Our findings suggest that, even as long-context evaluation shifts from simple retrieval toward complex reasoning, accurate grounding in relevant evidence remains an indispensable capability with substantial room for improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [HARQ for Slow Fluid Antenna Multiple Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18426)
*Sixu Han,Kai-Kit Wong,Hanjiang Hong,Hyundong Shin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出HARQ辅助的慢速流体天线多址接入（HARQ-sFAMA）框架，在每次HARQ回合中用户选择不同端口并合并多轮信号，理论分析表明其在可靠性、时延和能量效率上显著优于一次性传输的sFAMA方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sFAMA研究仅关注一次性传输的物理层性能，未探索其与重传协议的交互及网络层性能，亟需弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出HARQ-sFAMA框架，每轮HARQ用户选择不同FAS端口并合并信号；建立解析模型刻画中断概率、平均分组等待时间和能量效率。

Result: 数值结果验证理论分析，HARQ-sFAMA在可靠性、时延和能量效率上全面优于传统一次性sFAMA。

Conclusion: HARQ与sFAMA的融合为未来无线网络提供了一条实用且兼容标准的海量接入途径。

Abstract: Slow fluid antenna multiple access (sFAMA), enabled by the fluid antenna system (FAS), has recently emerged as a practical and low-complexity paradigm for supporting massive wireless connectivity. While existing studies have characterized its physical-layer performance under one-shot transmission, its interaction with retransmission protocols and the resulting networking performance remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we study a downlink hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ)-assisted sFAMA framework, termed HARQ-sFAMA, in which each user performs distinguished port selection in every HARQ round and combines the received signals across multiple rounds to improve decoding reliability. We develop a comprehensive analytical framework to characterize the outage probability, average packet waiting time, and energy efficiency of the proposed system. The analysis reveals how HARQ exploits the spatial reconfigurability of FAS to simultaneously enhance reliability and improve queueing performance. Numerical results corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the HARQ-sFAMA system significantly outperforms conventional one-shot sFAMA in terms of reliability, delay, and energy efficiency. These findings suggest that the integration of HARQ and sFAMA provides a promising pathway toward a practical and standards-compatible massive access solution for future wireless networks.

</details>


### [128] [Cyclic and Constacyclic Codes Over Z4+iZ4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18471)
*Miguel Martín,Ekin Özman*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限链环R=Z4+iZ4（其中i^2=-1）上的循环码和常循环码，证明了R上的所有常循环码都等价于循环码，提出了一种生成R上所有单根常循环码生成元的算法，并通过Gray映射从R上的常循环码得到线性Z4码，发现了新的最佳线性Z4码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限链环上研究循环码和常循环码是编码理论中的重要课题，特别是Z4+iZ4环上的码类具有丰富的代数结构，探索其性质并寻找新的最优码具有理论和应用价值。

Method: 首先证明R上的所有常循环码等价于循环码；然后提出算法生成所有单根常循环码的生成元；最后通过Gray映射将R上的常循环码转化为线性Z4码。

Result: 证明了常循环码与循环码的等价性，给出了生成元的算法，并通过该方法发现了新的最佳线性Z4码。

Conclusion: 该研究为有限链环R上的码类构造提供了新方法，所发现的新最优Z4码展示了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study cyclic and constacyclic codes over the finite chain ring R=Z4+iZ4, where i^2=-1. We prove that all constacyclic codes over R are equivalent to cyclic codes. An algorithm to obtain generators for all simple root constacyclic codes over R is presented. Using a Gray map we then obtain linear Z4 codes from constacyclic codes over R. We present new best linear Z4 codes found using this method.

</details>


### [129] [Task-Oriented Wave Processing with Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces: Framework, Fusion, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18612)
*Qiao Qi,Qiyu Chen,Jiancheng An,Xiaoming Chen,Zhaohui Yang,Chongwen Huang,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出利用堆叠智能超表面（SIM）在物理层实现计算范式，将无线环境从被动媒介转变为可编程信号处理器，以解决6G网络中多业务深度融合带来的性能冲突问题，通过统一框架将高层业务需求直接映射到波域合成，实现从简单共存到真正业务共生的演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中多样化业务的深度集成对传统任务无关信道构成巨大挑战，常导致性能冲突，亟需新的物理层范式来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于堆叠智能超表面（SIM）的物理层计算范式，建立统一框架将高层业务需求直接映射到波域合成，利用SIM的深度计算架构实现通感一体化（ISAC）和通算一体化（ICC）场景下的资源冲突消解。

Result: 数值结果验证了该方法能够使系统从简单的业务共存提升为真正的业务共生，有效解决资源冲突问题。

Conclusion: SIM赋能的物理层计算范式为实现业务原生的6G架构提供了可行路径，并指出了衍射信道建模和逆任务到相位映射等关键研究方向。

Abstract: The deep integration of diverse services in sixth-generation (6G) networks poses significant challenges to conventional task-agnostic channels, often resulting in performance conflicts. To resolve these bottlenecks, this article introduces a physical-layer computing paradigm enabled by stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs), transforming the wireless environment from a passive medium into a programmable signal processor. Specifically, we establish a unified framework to map high-level service requirements directly to wave-domain synthesis. We then investigate the fusion of diverse services, demonstrating how the deep computational architecture of SIMs resolves resource conflicts in integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) and integrated communication and computation (ICC) scenarios. Furthermore, we critically analyze fundamental challenges, including diffractive channel modeling and inverse task-to-phase mapping, while validating through numerical results that this approach elevates the system from simple coexistence to true service symbiosis. Finally, we discuss key research directions to pave the way for service-native 6G architectures.

</details>


### [130] [Distributed Edge Learning under Imperfect Data Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18649)
*Mehdi Karbalayghareh,David J. Love,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式学习系统中，传感噪声（受模态、分辨率、功率和样本量影响）对学习收敛的影响，提出最优模态选择应最小化噪声-梯度对齐而非总噪声功率，并联合优化了模态、分辨率、功率和样本数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式学习系统假设客户端本地数据质量固定，但实际中数据通过不完美的物理过程感知，质量受模态、分辨率、传感功率和样本量影响。亟需建模传感噪声对学习收敛的影响并实现多维度联合优化。

Method: 将传感噪声建模为结构化、模态依赖的协方差矩阵；推导非凸学习收敛界，其中不可约的感知底限由模态噪声协方差与损失敏感度几何之间的对齐程度决定；推导传感器硬件的可达界和饱和阈值；联合优化模态、分辨率、功率和样本数量。

Result: 最优模态选择应最小化噪声-梯度对齐而非总噪声功率；获得传感器硬件关于ε-驻点的可达界以及累积数据集大小的硬件饱和阈值；仿真验证了联合优化策略的性能增益。

Conclusion: 在分布式学习系统中，传感噪声的管理应关注噪声与损失敏感度几何的对齐程度，而非单纯降低总噪声功率；多维度联合优化能有效提升学习性能。

Abstract: Distributed learning systems typically assume that local data is already available at clients with fixed quality, while in practice, data is sensed through imperfect physical processes whose quality depends on modality, resolution, sensing power, and sample size. We model sensing noise as a structured, modality-dependent covariance and derive a non-convex learning convergence bound whose irreducible sensing floor is governed by the alignment between the modality noise covariance and the loss-sensitivity geometry. Thus, the optimal modality minimizes this noise-gradient alignment rather than total noise power alone. The analysis further yields a sensor-hardware achievability bound for epsilon-stationarity and a hardware-saturation threshold on the accumulated dataset size. We jointly optimize modality, resolution, power, and sample count and demonstrate the performance gain through simulations.

</details>


### [131] [Distribution of $k$-th Maximum Order Statistics of Independent, & Non-Identical SNR random variables in $κ-μ$ fading and its applications in $6G$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18723)
*Srinivas Sagar,Athira Subhash,Sheetal Kalyani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用极值理论推导了κ-μ衰落信道下信噪比（SNR）第k次最大顺序统计量的渐近分布，涵盖了Rice、Rayleigh和Nakagami-m等特殊分布，并在MIMO天线选择、反向散射系统、智能反射面和无人机中继选择等应用中验证了其实用价值，同时推导了中断概率和平均吞吐量的表达式，蒙特卡洛仿真验证了结果的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立κ-μ衰落信道下SNR第k次最大顺序统计量的渐近分布，以统一框架涵盖Rice、Rayleigh和Nakagami-m等常见分布，并解决实际通信系统中的性能分析需求。

Method: 采用极值理论分析κ-μ衰落信道中独立非同分布参数下的SNR顺序统计量；推导第k次最大顺序统计量的渐近分布，并据此计算中断概率和平均吞吐量；通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证理论结果的准确性。

Result: 得到了κ-μ衰落信道下SNR第k次最大顺序统计量的渐近分布表达式，以及中断概率和平均吞吐量的解析结果；蒙特卡洛仿真证实了推导结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 该研究为κ-μ衰落信道下的顺序统计量分析提供了统一的理论框架，所获结果可广泛应用于MIMO天线选择、反向散射系统、智能反射面和无人机中继选择等现代无线通信技术中。

Abstract: This paper employs extreme value theory to establish the asymptotic distribution of the $k$-th maximum order statistics of signal to noise ratio (SNR) for a $κ-μ$ fading channel with independent and non-identically distributed (i.n.i.d.) parameters. Since $κ-μ$ encompasses well-known distributions such as Rice, Rayleigh, and Nakagami-m, the order statistics for these are also derived as special cases. We demonstrate the practical significance of our results by showcasing their applicability in several applications, including antenna selection in MIMO systems, backscatter systems, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces, and UAV-assisted relay selection systems. Furthermore, by utilizing the $k$-th maximum order statistics, we derive expressions for outage probability and average throughput of $k$-th maximum order statistics. Comprehensive Monte Carlo simulations are carried out to verify the accuracy of the proposed results.

</details>


### [132] [Flexible Intelligent Metasurface-Aided ISAC: User Fairness Optimization and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18893)
*Hailun Huang,Yuwen Cao,Jiguang He,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对柔性智能超表面（FIM）与非正交多址（NOMA）辅助的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，研究最大化-最小用户公平性优化问题，提出一种联合设计基站发射波束赋形、FIM反射系数和表面形变的交替优化算法，实现了通信与感知性能的优越权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISAC设计存在多用户干扰、性能不均衡以及感知精度被忽视的问题，亟需一种能兼顾用户公平性与感知精度的联合优化框架。

Method: 推导了目标感知中离开角（AoD）估计的闭式克拉美-罗下界（CRLB），将其嵌入最大-最小公平性优化框架；通过交替优化（AO）算法联合求解基站发射波束赋形、FIM反射系数和表面形变这一非凸问题。

Result: 仿真验证所提方案显著提升了用户公平性，有效平衡了通信性能与感知精度，并揭示了信干噪比（SINR）与感知CRLB之间的耦合特性。

Conclusion: 该工作为6G网络中FIM辅助ISAC系统优化提供了可行方案，实现了频谱资源在通信与感知任务间的优越性能权衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates max-min user fairness optimization for flexible intelligent metasurface (FIM) and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems with active self-localization. To tackle the multi-user interference, performance imbalance, and neglected sensing accuracy problems encountered in conventional ISAC designs, we derive the closed-form Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for angle-of-departure (AoD) estimation in target sensing and embed it into a max-min fairness optimization framework. The optimization problem, which jointly designs the base station transmit beamforming, FIM reflection coefficients, and surface deformation, is non-convex and solved by an alternating optimization (AO) algorithm. Notably, the devised optimization framework facilitates superior performance trade-off in terms of spectrum resource utilization between communication and sensing tasks. Simulation results validate that the proposed scheme significantly improves user fairness, balances communication performance and sensing precision effectively, and reveals the coupling characteristic between signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and sensing CRLB. This work provides a feasible solution for FIM-aided ISAC system optimization in 6G networks.

</details>


### [133] [Function-Correcting RT Codes: A Coding Framework for Parallel Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18908)
*Huiying Liu,Hongwei Liu,Sihem Mesnager*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出函数纠正RT码(FCRTCs)，将函数纠正码从Hamming度量推广到Rosenbloom-Tsfasman度量(RT度量)。通过引入不规则RT距离矩阵码，建立了FCRTCs最优冗余优化与这类码构造之间的直接对应关系。针对任意函数给出了最优冗余的通用上下界，并针对RT权重函数、RT权重分布函数和RT局部二值函数等特定函数类得到了更紧的界。对于RT权重分布函数给出了显式构造，并在多种情况下证明了其达到最优冗余界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数纠正码能够有效降低编码冗余，但其研究局限于Hamming度量；RT度量因其与并行信道通信的相关性而受到广泛关注。本文将函数纠正码从Hamming度量推广到更一般的RT度量，以在并行信道场景下实现冗余高效的函数恢复。

Method: 引入一类新的矩阵码——不规则RT距离码，建立FCRTCs冗余优化问题与该类码构造之间的直接对应关系，从而形成统一的冗余界推导框架。针对不同函数类利用其结构特性推导更紧的冗余界。

Result: 建立了任意函数下FCRTCs最优冗余的通用上下界；针对RT权重函数、RT权重分布函数和RT局部二值函数得到了更紧的界；给出了RT权重分布函数的显式构造并在多种情况下证明了达到最优冗余界。

Conclusion: 该框架将函数纠正码理论显著推广到RT度量，为研究并行信道上的可靠通信提供了新的理论工具，并为超越经典Hamming设置的冗余高效编码开辟了新视角。

Abstract: Function-correcting codes have recently emerged as an effective approach to reducing coding redundancy when the objective is to reliably recover only the prescribed function values of the transmitted information, rather than the complete message itself. As a natural generalization of the Hamming metric, the Rosenbloom--Tsfasman metric (RT-metric) has attracted considerable attention due to its relevance to communication over parallel channels. Motivated by these two research directions, we introduce in this paper function-correcting RT codes (FCRTCs), which extend the framework of function-correcting codes from the Hamming metric to the more general RT metric.
  To investigate the fundamental problem of optimal redundancy, we introduce a new class of matrix codes, called irregular RT-distance codes, and establish a direct correspondence between the redundancy optimization problem for FCRTCs and the construction of such codes. This connection provides a unified framework for deriving redundancy bounds under the RT metric.
  For arbitrary functions, we establish general upper and lower bounds on the optimal redundancy of FCRTCs. We then specialize our analysis to several important classes of functions, namely RT-weight functions, RT-weight distribution functions, and RT-locally-two-valued binary functions, for which substantially sharper bounds are obtained by exploiting their specific structural properties. Furthermore, we present explicit constructions of FCRTCs for RT-weight distribution functions and prove that these constructions attain the optimal redundancy bounds in several cases.
  The proposed framework considerably extends the theory of function-correcting codes to the RT metric, provides new theoretical tools for studying reliable communication over parallel channels, and opens new perspectives for redundancy-efficient coding beyond the classical Hamming setting.

</details>


### [134] [A Griesmer-Type Bound for List-Decodable Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18941)
*Shengwei Liu,Chunyan Qin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了$(τ,L)$-列表可解码码的列表解码半径上限与广义Hamming权重之间的关系，推导出适用于q元线性码的最小距离下界，并结合Griesmer界给出块长下界。针对$q=3^a$构造了一族达到该Griesmer型界的最优码，证明该界在某些参数下严格优于Singleton型界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Singleton型界限制了固定列表大小时的列表解码半径与码率关系，但可能存在其他约束条件。本文旨在探索广义Hamming权重对列表解码半径的约束，并寻找可能优于Singleton型界的界限。

Method: 利用广义Hamming权重导出列表解码半径的上界；结合经典Griesmer界推导块长的下界；对于$q=3^a$情形，通过显式构造$[q+3,2,q+1]$线性码验证所提界的紧致性。

Result: 对$1\le L\le q-1$，每个$(τ,L)$-列表可解码的q元线性码的最小距离至少为$τ+\lfloor τ/L\rfloor+1$。构造的$[q+3,2,q+1]$码是$(2q/3,2)$-列表可解码的，且达到Griesmer型界，但不满足Singleton型界。

Conclusion: Griesmer型界在某些参数范围内可以成为Singleton型界的严格改进，为列表可解码码的设计提供了新的理论界限和构造方法。

Abstract: A code $C\subseteq F_q^n$ is $(τ,L)$-list-decodable if every Hamming ball of radius $τ$ contains at most $L$ codewords of $C$. Here $τ$ is the list-decoding radius, and $L$ is the list size. Singleton-type bounds constrain the radius and the rate when $L$ is fixed. These bounds are not the only possible constraints on list-decodable codes. In this paper, we derive an upper bound on the list-decoding radius in terms of generalized Hamming weights. As a consequence, for $1\le L\le q-1$, every $(τ,L)$-list-decodable $q$-ary linear code has minimum distance at least $ τ+\left\lfloor \fracτ{L}\right\rfloor+1. $ Combining this lower bound with the classical Griesmer bound gives a Griesmer-type lower bound on the block length. For $q=3^a$, we construct an explicit family of $q$-ary linear $[q+3,2,q+1]$ codes. These codes are $(2q/3,2)$-list-decodable and meet the Griesmer-type bound with equality. They do not attain the Singleton-type bound. Thus the Griesmer-type bound can be a strict improvement over the Singleton-type bound.

</details>


### [135] [On the Performance of Fluid Antenna Systems under Block-Diagonal Correlation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19039)
*Jiangsheng Huangfu,Zhengyu Song,Tianwei Hou,Anna Li,Kai Kit Wong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了采用最大比合并（MRC）的单输入多输出（SIMO）流体天线系统（FAS），在块对角相关模型下推导了精确中断概率和遍历速率的闭合表达式，证明了分集阶数近似等于端口数，且块划分仅影响编码增益而不影响分集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAS作为一种新兴的可重构天线技术，能够利用紧凑孔径内的细粒度空间信道变化，但现有理论分析尚不充分，特别是针对实际相关信道模型（如块对角结构）下的性能表征有待完善。

Method: 采用块对角相关模型，将FAS端口划分为独立块并在每块内选择最强端口进行MRC合并；通过卷积和特征函数形式推导精确中断概率，利用高信噪比渐近分析获得分集阶数，并基于Gamma匹配和Jensen不等式推导遍历速率的闭合近似表达式。

Result: 分集阶数近似等于端口总数，与块划分方式无关；增加端口数或块数均可提升系统性能；所提SIMO-FAS在使用更少合并支路的情况下，可实现与传统MRC接收机相当或更优的中断性能。

Conclusion: 块对角结构下的SIMO-FAS在分集性能上仅取决于端口总数而非块数，这使得块划分可以在不影响分集的前提下被优化以提升编码增益；FAS技术在实际无线网络中具有显著的应用潜力。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FASs) have recently emerged as a promising reconfigurable antenna technology for future wireless networks, owing to their unique ability to exploit fine-grained spatial channel variations within a compact aperture. In this paper, a single-input multiple-output (SIMO) FAS employing maximum-ratio combining (MRC) is investigated under the block-diagonal correlation model, where the ports of FAS are partitioned into independent blocks and the strongest port within each block is selected for MRC combining. Exact outage probability (OP) expressions are first derived in both convolution and characteristic-function forms. To gain further insights, closed-form high-SNR asymptotic expressions are developed, from which the diversity order is shown to approximate the number of ports. This result reveals that block partitioning influences only the coding gain and can therefore be optimized without compromising the diversity performance. For the ergodic rate (ER), a Gamma-matching approximation together with a tighter Jensen-based approximation is derived in closed form. Simulation results corroborate the analytical framework and demonstrate that: i) increasing either the number of ports or the number of blocks improves the system performance; ii) the diversity order depends solely on the number of ports; and iii) the proposed SIMO-FAS achieves comparable or superior outage performance to conventional MRC receivers despite employing fewer combining branches.

</details>


### [136] [Secure OFDM-IM ISAC With Artificial-Noise-Aided Index Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19068)
*Ufuk Altun,Onur Günlü*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于人工噪声辅助的OFDM-IM安全框架，用于集成感知与通信。在感知目标同时也是潜在窃听者的场景下，将人工噪声注入非活跃子载波，通过预编码器在合法接收端消除噪声，同时向目标投射活跃子载波能量以干扰其索引检测。通过优化问题在保证安全通信的同时最大化向目标的发射功率，数值结果表明相比传统OFDM方案提升了目标定向发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成感知与通信系统中，当感知目标同时也是潜在窃听者时，传统方法会抑制朝向目标的信号功率以保障通信安全，但这会削弱感知性能。需要一种既能实现安全通信又能保持对目标有效感知的新方案。

Method: 1) 采用OFDM-IM框架，将人工噪声注入非活跃子载波；2) 设计预编码器，在合法接收端空间上消除人工噪声，同时将活跃子载波的能量级别投射到目标方向；3) 利用单站ISAC发射器已知人工噪声波形，非活跃子载波也用于感知；4) 将该场景建模为在保证安全通信约束下最大化向目标发射功率的优化问题。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的方案相比传统OFDM方案，显著提升了向目标的定向发射功率，同时保证了与合法接收端的安全通信。

Conclusion: 该AN辅助的OFDM-IM安全框架有效解决了ISAC系统中感知目标同时为窃听者的安全-感知权衡问题，通过智能利用非活跃子载波注入人工噪声，实现了通信安全性与感知性能的双赢。

Abstract: We propose an artificial noise (AN)-aided, secure OFDM with index modulation (OFDM-IM) framework for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Consider a security-critical scenario, where the sensing target is also a potential eavesdropper. Instead of suppressing the signal power toward the target, the proposed method injects AN into inactive OFDM-IM subcarriers. The precoder spatially nulls the AN at the legitimate receiver, while projecting active-subcarrier energy levels toward the target to deceive its index detector. Since the monostatic ISAC transmitter knows the AN waveform, the same inactive subcarriers also contribute to sensing. We formulate this scenario as an optimization problem that maximizes transmit power toward the target while providing secure communication with a legitimate receiver. Numerical results show that the proposed scheme improves targeted transmit power compared to classical OFDM.

</details>


### [137] [Squeezing the Most Out of Preemption for AoI Minimization: Single-source Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19216)
*Nail Akar,Mohammad Moltafet,Sennur Ulukus,Marian Codreanu,Roy D. Yates*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了单源单服务器连续时间状态更新系统中的信息年龄优化问题，针对基于服务中更新年龄的概率抢占策略，提出了平均AoI和峰值AoI的通用分析方法，并优化了两种具体策略：固定概率抢占(PP)和阈值抢占(TP)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对基于更新年龄的概率性抢占策略的通用分析方法，需要一种能够推导任意此类策略下平均AoI和峰值AoI的理论框架，并针对具体策略进行调优。

Method: 提出一种通用分析方法，推导任意基于更新年龄的抢占策略下平均AoI和平均峰值AoI的闭合表达式；利用一维线搜索对两种策略（固定概率抢占PP和阈值抢占TP）进行参数调优。

Result: 在对数正态分布服务时间下，验证了PP和TP策略调优的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分析方法能够有效评估和优化基于更新年龄的抢占策略，PP和TP策略经一维调优后在不同服务时间分布下均能改善信息年龄性能。

Abstract: In this work, we study a single-source single-server continuous-time status update system where the updates arrive according to a Poisson process and update service times are generally distributed. In our proposed setting, a preemption policy refers to one where a new update preempts the ongoing one with a probability depending on the age of the update in service. We first propose an analytical method to derive the average age of information (AoI) and average peak AoI (PAoI) for any such preemption policy. This analysis is then utilized to tune two particular preemption policies: (i) probabilistic preemption (PP), in which preemption takes place according to a fixed probability regardless of the update age, (ii) threshold-based preemption (TP), for which preemption is incurred when the update age exceeds a certain threshold, both using one-dimensional line search. The effectiveness of policy tuning for the PP and TP policies is validated using lognormal-distributed update service times.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [FinanceComplexQA: Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning on Industrial-grade Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19238)
*Xianfu Cheng,Shiwei Zhang,Jiyu Zhao,Jian Yang,Xinyuan Wang,Ming Zhou,Weixiao Zhou,Xiangyuan Guan,Xiang Li,Zhenhe Wu,Ziyi Ni,Zhoujun Li,Bingjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文设计了基于专家知识的金融文档合成技能 Finance-LaTeX SKILL，生成了2000份专业金融文档及6000个高质量问答对，并构建了金融文档开放生成基准 FinanceComplexQA（含2026个深度研究任务，覆盖1009份文档），用于评估RAG系统和智能体推理工具在数值计算、多跳推理、内容摘要和行业分析等方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在处理复杂现实金融问题时表现差异显著，缺乏专门针对金融文档复杂布局和深度推理能力的综合评估基准。

Method: 设计 Finance-LaTeX SKILL 技能，基于专家知识合成具有复杂布局的金融文档；构建基于该技能的智能体工作流，生成专业文档和问答对；提出 FinanceComplexQA 基准，包含双语支持、六大场景、七类任务，采用 Agent-as-a-Judge 进行多指标评估。

Result: 生成了2000份专业金融文档和6000个问答对；FinanceComplexQA 包含2026个深度研究任务，覆盖1009份金融文档；对主流RAG系统和智能体推理工具进行了全面评估，识别了在数值计算、多跳推理、内容摘要和行业分析等方面的失败案例。

Conclusion: FinanceComplexQA 能够有效评估智能体在金融文档深度推理方面的综合能力，通过失败案例分析揭示了现有系统在复杂金融任务中的不足。

Abstract: Agentic Reasoning has become a transformative force in financial analysis due to its ability to integrate large-scale information and generate reliable and accurate content. However, when handling complex real-world problems, different agents still show significant performance variation. In this work, we design Finance-LaTeX SKILL, a skill for synthesizing financial documents with complex layouts based on expert knowledge. Using an agent workflow built on this skill, we generate 2,000 professional financial documents along with 6,000 high-quality question-answer pairs. To evaluate the overall capability of agents, we introduce FinanceComplexQA, a comprehensive open-ended generation benchmark for financial documents that closely resembles real-world scenarios. It contains 2,026 deep research tasks targeting 1009 financial documents. FinanceComplexQA has 8 key features: bilingual support; coverage of six mainstream scenarios and seven tasks; expert-level document reasoning questions; deep research of complex layouts; relatively stable and permanent reference answers; and precise evaluation through an Agent-as-a-Judge with multiple evaluation metrics. Using FinanceComplexQA, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of leading RAG systems and agentic reasoning tools for financial document QA. Through identifying and analyzing failure cases, we provide an in-depth study of their capabilities in numerical computation, multi-hop reasoning, content summarization, and industry analysis.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient strain-space hyperreduction in large-deformation solid mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19330)
*Erik Faust,Lisa Scheunemann*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文将在计算均质化中表现优异的应变空间模型降阶技术推广到大变形固体力学问题。通过离线计算边界一致场满足任意参数化Dirichlet边界条件，提出了应变空间的ECM方法，并将E3C和EMSL推广到计算均质化之外。在超弹性算例中，应变空间方法在精度-效率权衡上优于位移空间方法，EMSL实现10万倍加速，E3C实现1万倍加速且精度极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变空间模型降阶技术在计算均质化中展现出卓越的运行时-精度权衡性能，但尚未被推广到大变形固体力学中更广泛的问题（如非均质化场景），且需要处理任意参数化Dirichlet边界条件。

Method: 通过离线构建边界一致场实现任意参数化Dirichlet边界条件的满足；将经验积分析方法(ECM)置于应变空间；将经验修正簇积分(E3C)和经验材料采样与线性化(EMSL)推广到计算均质化之外的问题。

Result: 在超弹性算例中，应变空间方法在运行时-精度权衡上优于位移空间的ECSW方法。E3C实现约10,000倍加速，EMSL实现约100,000倍加速且保持高精度。

Conclusion: EMSL是在线和离线时间预算非常有限时的首选方法；E3C在可接受稍多运行时的情况下能提供卓越精度；应变空间方法整体优于位移空间替代方案。

Abstract: Strain-space model order reduction (MOR) techniques have recently been shown to achieve exceptional performance in terms of the tradeoff between runtime and accuracy achieved in computational homogenisation problems. In this article, we generalise such techniques to problems in large-deformation solid mechanics beyond the context of computational homogenisation. Arbitrary-valued, parameterised Dirichlet boundary conditions are satisfied by construction using a lifting with boundary-consistent fields computed offline. This allows us to pose a version of the Empirical Cubature Method (ECM) [24,25] in strain space and generalise the Empirically Corrected Cluster Cubature (E3C) [46,48,49] as well as Empirical Material Sampling and Linearisation (EMSL) [17] beyond computational homogenisation problems.
  The strain-space versions of EMSL, ECM, and E3C are compared against each other and a standard displacement-space formulation of Energy Conserving Weighting and Sampling (ECSW) [15]. On two hyperelastic example problems with parameterised material behaviour and deformation, the strain-space methods outperform the displacement-space alternative in the tradeoff between runtime and accuracy. E3C and EMSL in particular facilitate 10,000 and 100,000-fold speedups, respectively, while retaining high levels of accuracy. EMSL is shown to be the method of choice when online and offline runtime budgets are very limited, while E3C yields exceptional levels of accuracy when slightly more runtime is acceptable.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [SysAdmin: Measuring Instrumental Power-Seeking in Frontier AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18239)
*Mana Azarm,Qiyao Wei,Rahul Nambiar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SysAdmin基准测试，将前沿大语言模型置于高保真Linux沙盒中作为系统管理员，测量其在五种维度上的权力寻求倾向。对七个前沿模型在四种实验条件下共2800个任务进行评估，经偏差校正后权力寻求率约为0-5%。正向控制实验达到100%检测率。结果表明当前模型在自然系统管理场景中极少自发表现出权力寻求行为，但存在更显著的其他失败模式（如规范博弈和抗拒目标修改）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权力寻求行为（即AI系统获取资源、逃避监督或抵抗终止等超出任务要求的行为）被识别为导致失控风险的关键因素，因此需要系统性地测量和评估前沿模型的权力寻求倾向。

Method: 构建SysAdmin基准测试，将前沿语言模型部署为高保真Linux沙盒环境中的自主系统管理员，分别从自我保存、增强自主性、资源获取、环境修改和策略性隐藏五个维度设计任务。在四种实验条件下评估七个前沿模型，共执行2800个任务，并使用人工标注校正数据进行偏差校正。

Result: 偏差校正后权力寻求率为0-5%；正向控制（明确权力寻求提示）达到100%检测率，验证了测量灵敏度。虽未发现显著的自发权力寻求行为，但观察到更突出的规范博弈和抗拒目标修改等其他失败模式。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在自然系统管理情境中表现出极少的自发权力寻求行为，但模型特定的失败模式表明评估仍需覆盖多种错位模式。权力寻求风险较低，但其他类型的安全问题（如规范博弈）更值得关注。

Abstract: Power-seeking defined as behaviors where AI systems acquire resources, evade oversight, or resist termination beyond task requirements is identified as a key driver of Loss of Control (LoC) risk. In this work, we introduce SysAdmin, a benchmark that positions frontier language models as autonomous system administrators in a high-fidelity Linux sandbox to measure power-seeking propensity across five dimensions: self-preservation, increasing autonomy, resource acquisition, environment modification, and strategic concealment. We evaluated seven frontier models across four experimental conditions in a total of 2800 tasks. After bias correction using human-annotated calibration data, corrected power-seeking estimates ranged from 0 to about 5 percent per model. We also conducted a positive control with explicit power-seeking prompts that achieved 100% detection, validating measurement sensitivity. Our findings indicate current frontier models exhibit minimal spontaneous power-seeking in naturalistic system administration contexts, though model-specific failure modes suggest evaluations must test diverse misalignment patterns. Nevertheless, we discovered other more pronounced failure modes (than power-seeking) such as specification gaming and resistance to goal modification.

</details>


### [141] [Calibrated Selective Fact-Checking via Evidence Chain Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18240)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据链评估（ECE）框架，通过允许模型在证据不足时选择弃权（不确定判决），解决了大语言模型在事实核查中即使证据薄弱也强制做出二元判断的可靠性问题。ECE在ECE-Bench上达到91.6%标准准确率、93.7%覆盖率和97.8%选择性准确率，弃权案例主要集中在低可靠性证据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM事实核查系统强制输出真/假二元判决，但在支持证据薄弱、稀疏或内部不一致时仍会给出自信判断，导致严重的可靠性问题。需要一种允许模型在证据不足时选择弃权的选择性事实核查框架。

Method: 提出证据链评估（ECE）框架，一种选择性事实核查方法，允许系统通过不确定判决（abstention）替代强制真/假决策。系统作为工具使用型验证代理，通过网页搜索、学术搜索和可执行检查收集证据，返回包含置信度和源级元数据的结构化判决。

Result: 在ECE-Bench上，ECE达到91.6%标准准确率、93.7%覆盖率和97.8%选择性准确率。在95个案例中弃权6例（5/6位于源级L4低可靠性证据设置）。未在ECE、Brier分数或AURC等聚合校准指标上超越最强检索基线，但在选择性预测权衡上表现清晰。

Conclusion: 弃权机制作为面向安全的处理认知薄弱证据的有效手段，在低可靠性证据场景下集中弃权验证了该方法作为安全导向机制的有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can achieve strong fact-checking accuracy, yet forced binary decisions conceal a critical reliability problem: systems may issue confident verdicts even when supporting evidence is weak, sparse, or internally inconsistent. We address this issue through Evidence Chain Evaluation (ECE), a selective fact-checking framework that permits abstention via an uncertain verdict instead of requiring a true/false decision for every claim. The evaluated system is a tool-using verification agent that gathers evidence through web search, scholarly search, and executable checks, and then returns a structured verdict with confidence and source-level metadata. On ECE-Bench, ECE achieves 91.6% standard accuracy, 93.7% coverage, and 97.8% selective accuracy on answered claims. Although ECE does not outperform the strongest retrieval baseline on aggregate calibration metrics such as Expected Calibration Error, Brier score, or AURC, it delivers a clear selective-prediction trade-off: the system maintains very high accuracy on answered claims while deferring 6 of 95 cases. These deferred cases are concentrated in lower-reliability evidence settings (5/6 at source level L4), supporting the view that abstention functions as a safety-oriented mechanism for handling epistemically weak evidence. Code is available at https://github.com/ cheshireyang/ECE.git

</details>


### [142] [BatchDAG: LLM-Planned Execution Graphs for Scalable Ad-Hoc Analysis Over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18241)
*Anupreet Walia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BatchDAG 是一个让 LLM 生成有类型有向无环图（DAG）操作的工作流编排系统，通过拓扑波并行、结构化 JSON 数据流和实体感知批处理，在大规模企业数据集上实现跨实体分析，减少了最多 47 倍 LLM 调用，在 5 万以上会议的查询中 60 秒内完成，成本仅 $0.02–$0.24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型擅长分析单个文档，但在处理企业级数据集的跨实体穷举分析时，面临上下文溢出、实体归因丢失和顺序工具调用导致线性延迟等问题。现有方法如手工程序化管道缺乏通用性，而 ReAct 等智能体方法效率低且可归因性差。

Method: BatchDAG 由 LLM 生成一个带有类型的 DAG，节点包含 SQL 查询、语义搜索、内存变换、并行扇出和单次分析等操作，由确定性引擎按拓扑波并行执行，数据以结构化 JSON 流传递。核心优化是实体感知批处理：在扇出前按逻辑实体对行进行分组，将多次 LLM 调用合并为一次。

Result: 在 12 个转录密集查询上，BatchDAG（3.74/5）质量与专家设计的管道（3.25/5）相当，显著优于 ReAct 智能体（3.09/5, p<0.01）；归因能力领先（77% 转录证据率 vs 基线 46–60%）；结构化 JSON 中间表示相比散文摘要减少 27% 幻觉；规划器 300 次调用中 98.8% 生成合法 DAG；在生产中处理 5万+ 会议查询，延迟低于 60 秒，每次查询成本 $0.02–$0.24。

Conclusion: BatchDAG 是一个通用编排层，能够从自然语言自动生成合适的执行策略，替代多个手工程序化工作流。它在保持与专家管道相当质量的同时，显著提升了效率、可扩展性和可归因性，适用于大规模企业级跨文档分析场景。
