Existing algorithms are based on strategy iteration, which repeatedly computes optimal strategies for one player while fixing the other, leading to high computational cost. Our algorithm instead operates directly on a lattice-theoretic characterization of winning probabilities, exploiting structural properties of (almost-sure qualitative) winning states under parity objectives. We prove correctness and convergence of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [214] [Breaking Network Densification Limits with Distributed Cooperative Massive Access (DCMA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18492)
*Christoforos I. Dallas,Apostolos A. Tegos,Sotiris A. Tegos,Yue Xiao,George K. Karagiannidis,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式协作大规模接入（DCMA）框架在大规模网络中的性能，采用随机几何建模，提出了一种部分集中式无蜂窝云无线接入网络架构。针对该网络，设计了协同解码算法和基于博弈论的合并-分裂算法，仿真表明所提框架在中断概率上显著优于未实现SIC或RRH协作的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模网络场景下，传统集中式接入架构面临回程开销和计算复杂度挑战，而分布式协作接入中如何有效利用RRH间的协作与干扰消除尚缺乏系统性研究，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 引入随机几何对大规模网络进行建模；提出一种协同解码算法以解决用户分配与消息共享路由问题；基于博弈论设计合并-分裂算法，采用词典序偏好以最小化RRH使用数量同时保证性能。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提框架在中断概率方面显著优于未实现SIC或不利用RRH间协作的系统，验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的DCMA框架结合协同解码与合并-分裂算法，能够在大规模网络中有效利用RRH协作和SIC技术，显著降低中断概率，提升了系统性能。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the performance of the distributed cooperative massive access (DCMA) framework in large-scale network setups by incorporating stochastic geometry modeling. A partially centralized cell-free cloud-radio access (C-RAN) architecture is considered where remote radio heads (RRHs) decode transmitted messages and cooperate with each other to enhance system performance. Specifically, they can share decoded messages via feedback links, allowing receivers to cancel inter-user interference through successive interference cancellation (SIC), thus improving the decoding capabilities of the system. For such a network, we propose a novel synergetic decoding algorithm that efficiently resolves the assignment and message sharing routing for each user while accounting for practical network constraints. Furthermore, using game theory, we develop a merge-and-split algorithm with lexicographic preference to solve the problem of minimizing the RRHs utilized without compromising the performance. Simulation results show that the proposed framework significantly outperforms systems that do not implement SIC or take advantage of the cooperation between RRHs in terms of outage probability. Finally, we evaluate the performance of the proposed algorithms and validate their efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [Hospitals/Residents with Inseparable Couples: Finding a Coalition-Stable Assignment Is NP-Hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18634)
*Zeyuan Hu,C. Gregory Plaxton*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Rodríguez和Manlove在课程分配中关于联盟稳定性（coalition stability）计算复杂性的开放问题，证明在Hospitals/Residents with Sizes (HRS)问题中寻找联盟稳定分配是NP难的，即使医院容量和居民规模最多为2时仍然成立，并将该NP难结果推广到新的"单位联盟稳定性"（unitwise-coalition stability）概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rodríguez和Manlove在课程分配研究中考虑了四种稳定性概念（包括两种联盟概念），其中联盟稳定性（coalition stability）的计算复杂性是开放问题。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，并进一步探索更自然的联盟稳定性概念。

Method: 基于McDermid和Manlove的思想，通过归约方法证明HRS中寻找联盟稳定分配是NP难的；将NP难结果推广到HRS的特殊情况HRIC；引入新的"单位联盟稳定性"概念并证明相同NP难结果成立。

Result: 证明在HRS中寻找联盟稳定分配是NP难的，且当医院容量和居民规模≤2时仍然成立；该NP难结果适用于HRIC；新引入的单位联盟稳定性同样为NP难。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Rodríguez和Manlove关于联盟稳定性的开放问题，证实其计算复杂性为NP难，并引入了新的稳定性概念。

Abstract: In recent work on course allocation, Rodríguez and Manlove consider the complexity of finding a stable assignment under four notions of stability, including two coalitional notions. In one case, which they call pair-size stability, they show that a stable assignment always exists and they provide a polynomial-time algorithm to find one. In a second case, called pair stability, they observe that an earlier NP-hardness result of McDermid and Manlove holds for a special case of course allocation called Hospitals/Residents with Sizes ($\mbox{HRS}$). In a third case, called first-coalition stability, they use a reduction from $\mbox{HRS}$ to show it is NP-hard to find a stable assignment. They leave open the complexity of finding a stable assignment under so-called coalition stability. Building on ideas from McDermid and Manlove, we resolve the open problem of Rodríguez and Manlove by showing that it is NP-hard to find a coalition-stable assignment for $\mbox{HRS}$. Indeed, our proof shows that the problem remains NP-hard when the hospital capacities and resident sizes are at most two. Accordingly, our NP-hardness result applies to the special case of $\mbox{HRS}$ known as Hospitals/Residents with Inseparable Couples ($\mbox{HRIC}$). Finally, we introduce a novel and natural notion of coalitional stability for both $\mbox{HRS}$ and course allocation, and we show that our NP-hardness result extends to this notion, which we call unitwise-coalition stability.

</details>


### [216] [Stability in Combinatorial Markets with Side Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19098)
*Alexander Grosz,Chiara Vanoli*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文在组合市场模型中引入显式旁支付（side payments），允许代理人子集间的受限效用转移，从而捕捉不同形式的金融合谋。通过系统分类，发现高阶市场设置在买方间允许旁支付时本质等价，卖方间也存在类似现象，但二阶与三阶设置之间存在根本性结构分离。文中还提出了基于分区的受限可转移性概念（T-核），并分析了不同设置下的稳定性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组合市场模型（Bikhchandani & Ostroy, 2002; Bichler & Waldherr, 2017）未考虑代理人子集间的受限效用转移，无法刻画金融合谋等现实市场行为。作者旨在通过引入显式旁支付扩展模型，并系统分类不同市场设置的表征能力，理解其稳定性特性。

Method: （1）在经典组合市场模型基础上引入显式旁支付，定义不同代理人子集间的受限效用转移；（2）对大量看似不同的市场设置进行系统分类，比较其表征能力；（3）提出基于分区的受限可转移性概念（T-核），将经典TU和NTU稳定性泛化到部分可转移效用场景；（4）通过构造市场实例，分析不同设置下稳定性的关系与差异。

Result: （1）高阶市场设置在买方间允许旁支付时本质等价，卖方间也存在类似等价性；（2）二阶与三阶设置之间存在根本性结构分离；（3）在个性化定价下，所有稳定性概念均坍缩为NTU稳定性；（4）部分可转移效用可以在某些市场中产生比完全可转移或不可转移效用更好的稳定结果。

Conclusion: 旁支付的引入揭示了组合市场中不同设置间的深层结构关系：高阶设置存在等价性，而二阶与三阶之间存在根本性鸿沟。T-核为分析部分可转移效用下的稳定性提供了统一框架，个性化定价导致稳定性概念坍缩的发现对市场设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Combinatorial markets provide a general framework for trading bundles of indivisible goods. Building on the combinatorial market models of Bikhchandani & Ostroy (2002) and Bichler & Waldherr (2017), we introduce explicit side payments, thereby allowing restricted transfers of utility among subsets of agents and capturing different forms of financial collusion.
  This extension results in a large number of seemingly distinct market settings. However, we establish a systematic classification of their expressive power. Most notably, we show that higher-order market settings (i.e., those with more personalized prices and less clearinghouse power) are essentially equivalent in terms of market properties when side payments are permitted between buyers. An analogous collapse occurs for the third- and higher-order settings when side payments are permitted between sellers. In contrast, we identify a fundamental structural separation between the second- and third-order settings.
  To analyze stability in these environments, we generalize the classical concepts of stability with and without transferable utility (TU and NTU) to a partition-based notion of restricted transferability (the $T$-core). We relate stable outcomes across different settings to appropriate stability notions, identify market instances in which partially transferable utility yields a better (or any) stable outcome, and show that under personalized pricing, all stability notions collapse to NTU-stability.

</details>
